      BEGIN 33953
      Integer parameter channel (IP)
      Name=Max_diffrential
      Description=
      Unit=%
      Mode selector=Not used
      Default mode={70}
        Default value=70
      X=400
      Y=130
      END 33953
      
      BEGIN 33954
      Math channel (MAC)
      Name=Left unfree
      Description=
      Unit=Not used
      Function selector=Not used
      X=620
      Y=170
        
        BEGIN 33958
        Function
        Name=Default function
        Description=
        Qcode expression:
          right := DMAC_EV_1_Right
          left := DMAC_EV_2_Left
          
          if (right > 0 and left < 0) or (right < 0 and left > 0) then
            res := 0
          elseif  Abs(right) <= Abs(left) then
            res := left
          elseif Abs(right) > Abs(left) then
            res := MaxOfAbs(right * Max_diffrential  / 100, left)
          endif
          
          
          Result := res
        END 33958
      END 33954

      BEGIN 34003
      Math channel (MAC)
      Name=Right unfree
      Description=
      Unit=Not used
      Function selector=Not used
      X=620
      Y=130
        
        BEGIN 34007
        Function
        Name=Default function
        Description=
        Qcode expression:
          right := DMAC_EV_1_Right
          left := DMAC_EV_2_Left
          
          if (right > 0 and left < 0) or (right < 0 and left > 0) then
            res := 0
          elseif  Abs(right) >= Abs(left) then
            res := right
          elseif Abs(right) < Abs(left) then
            res := MaxOfAbs(left * Max_diffrential  / 100, right)
          endif
          
          
          Result := res
        END 34007
      END 34003

          BEGIN 67208
          Function
          Name=Critical
          Description=System Temp reached a critical level and system will be shut down
          Qcode expression:
            static outputValue:= False
            
            If Persistant Water Temp >= Water °C Critical then
              outputValue := True  //switch on
            elseif Persistant Water Temp < (Water °C Critical - Water Temp Hysteresis Gap ) then
              outputValue := False  //switch off
            endif
            
            Result := OutputValue
            
          END 67208

        BEGIN 35843
        Math channel (MAC)
        Name=Speed Error Calcul
        Description=
        Unit=%
        Function selector=Engine type
        X=750
        Y=390
          
          BEGIN 35844
          Function
          Name=EB2 1.2L
          Description=
          Qcode expression:
            if Speed_RPM_needed  >  0 then
            Speed := 100 - (EB2.Engine speed EB2 / Speed_RPM_needed) * 100
            else
            Speed := 0
            endif
            
            if Speed >= 100 then
            Speed := 100
            endif
            if Speed <= 0 then
            Speed := 0
            endif
            Result := Speed
          END 35844
          
          BEGIN 63405
          Function
          Name=FORD 1.5L
          Description=
          Qcode expression:
            if Speed_RPM_needed  >  0 then
            Speed := 100 - (Ford.Engine speed Ford / Speed_RPM_needed) * 100
            else
            Speed := 0
            endif
            
            if Speed >= 100 then
            Speed := 100
            endif
            if Speed <= 0 then
            Speed := 0
            endif
            Result := Speed
          END 63405
        END 35843
